2699:+fn v73_build(out:[]u8,payload:[]u8)i32 {
2700:+    let parent:[]u8=nio_alloc(32);let previous:[]u8=nio_alloc(32);
2701:+    let world:[]u8=nio_alloc(96);let ingress:[]u8=nio_alloc(16);
2702:+    ns_sha256("LANE_D_REDUCED_CALLER",parent);
2703:+    if(world.len!=96 || ingress.len!=16){return 71;}
2704:+    let rc:i32=cl_checkpoint_begin(out,1,parent,previous);if(rc!=0){return rc;}
2705:+    let at:i32=CL_CHECKPOINT_HEADER;
2706:+    rc=cl_checkpoint_section(out,&at,1,payload);
2707:+    if(rc!=0){return 81;}
2708:+    if(at!=120+payload.len){return 82;}
2709:+    let tag:[]u8=nio_alloc(4);if(tag.len!=4){return 83;}
2710:+    let id:i32=2;
2711:+    while(id<=10){cl_put(tag,0,id as i64);rc=cl_checkpoint_section(out,&at,id,tag);if(rc!=0){nio_free(tag);return 84;}id=id+1;}
2712:+    nio_free(tag);
2713:+    rc=cl_checkpoint_section(out,&at,11,world);if(rc!=0){return 85;}
2714:+    rc=cl_checkpoint_section(out,&at,12,ingress);if(rc!=0){return 86;}
2715:+    rc=cl_checkpoint_finish(out,at);if(cl_is_status(rc)==1){return 87;}return rc;
2716:+}

2722:+    if(out.len!=300000 || payload.len!=245536 || parent.len!=32 || previous.len!=32 || world.len!=96 || ingress.len!=16){return 70;}
2723:+    let i:i32=0;while(i<payload.len){payload[i]=(i & 255) as u8;i=i+1;}
2724:+    payload[0]=67;payload[1]=76;payload[2]=65;payload[3]=49;
2725:+    i=0;while(i<world.len){world[i]=((i*3) & 255) as u8;i=i+1;}

5159:+`cl_checkpoint_begin/section/finish/commit/load` and descriptor-rooted storage.
5160:+Section 1 holds the ENTIRE N17 245536-byte packet, including its inner digest.
5161:+Sections 2–10 remain explicit engineering tags, not additional learned state.
5162:+Section 11 is the 96-byte world; section 12 is the 16-byte ingress stream.
5163:+The resulting outer transaction is 245924 bytes. No existing transport/world
5164:+source or original driver was edited. This does not replace the original driver
5165:+with a behavioral learner. The source packet is freshly generated from current
5166:+N17 `state_image_qual_v92.zag`, NOT from any historical receipt or canonical brain.
5167:+
5168:+`ld_commit` validates both layers before persistence. `ld_load` wraps the
5169:+existing outer loader with an additional staging buffer: inner validation must
5170:+pass before any caller-visible packet, world, or ingress bytes are copied.
5171:+Thus atomicity means whole-record validation/publication and unchanged output
5172:+on refusal. It does NOT assert atomic pathname rename or power-loss durability
5173:+beyond the existing file/root sync implementation. Bad fixture writers purposely
5174:+bypass admission to exercise refusal. Torn packet/checkpoint coverage is a
5175:+deterministic truncation fixture, not a newly injected power failure; the original
5176:+native supervisor's partial-write crash/refusal regression was also rerun.

7696:+fn ct_root_path()[]u8 {return "Research/R33_CONTINUING_LIFE_V1/LANE_D_20260915/BASELINE_RUNTIME";}
7697:+fn ct_binary()[]u8 {return "/Users/Shared/micah/Documents/TNN/TNN/Research/R33_CONTINUING_LIFE_V1/LANE_D_20260915/baseline_driver";}
7698:+fn ct_unit()i32 {
7699:+    let root:i64=cl_root(ct_root_path());if(root<0 || cl_is_status(root)==1){return 70;}
7700:+    let f:i32=ct_observation()+ct_world()+ct_checkpoint_unit()+ct_path_tests(root);nio_close(root);
7701:+    let usage:CLUsage=cl_usage();f=f+cl_check("usage_status",usage.status,0);f=f+cl_check("observed_rss_positive",(usage.rss_bytes>0) as i32,1);f=f+cl_check("observed_cpu_nonnegative",(usage.cpu_us>=0) as i32,1);
7702:+    _zag_print("CL_UNIT_FAILURES,");cl_number(f);_zag_println("");return (f!=0) as i32;
7703:+}
7704:+fn ct_write()i32 {
7705:+    let root:i64=cl_root(ct_root_path());if(root<0 || cl_is_status(root)==1){return 71;}
7706:+    let w:[]u8=nio_alloc(96);cw_init(w,1717);let a:CWOutcome=cw_step(w,1);let b:CWOutcome=cw_step(w,4);
7707:+    let data:[]u8=nio_alloc(4096);let parent:[]u8=nio_alloc(32);let previous:[]u8=nio_alloc(32);ns_sha256("ENGINEERING_FIXTURE_NOT_R27",parent);
7708:+    let n:i32=ct_checkpoint(data,w,parent,previous,1);let f:i32=cl_check("writer_encode",(n>0) as i32,1);
7709:+    if(n>0){f=f+cl_check("writer_commit",cl_checkpoint_commit(root,data[0..n],parent,previous,1),0);f=f+cl_check("writer_pending_action",cl_u32(w,44),2);}
7710:+    nio_close(root);nio_free(w);nio_free(data);nio_free(parent);nio_free(previous);return (f!=0) as i32;
7711:+}
7712:+fn ct_reload()i32 {
7713:+    let root:i64=cl_root(ct_root_path());if(root<0 || cl_is_status(root)==1){return 72;}
7714:+    let data:[]u8=nio_alloc(4096);let parent:[]u8=nio_alloc(32);let previous:[]u8=nio_alloc(32);ns_sha256("ENGINEERING_FIXTURE_NOT_R27",parent);
7715:+    let n:i32=cl_checkpoint_load(root,1,parent,previous,data);let f:i32=cl_check("fresh_process_load",(n>0) as i32,1);
7716:+    if(n>0){let w:[]u8=cl_checkpoint_get(data[0..n],11);f=f+cl_check("pending_after_process_restart",cl_u32(w,44),2);
7717:+        let pristine:[]u8=nio_alloc(96);cw_init(pristine,1717);let a:CWOutcome=cw_step(pristine,1);let b:CWOutcome=cw_step(pristine,4);
7718:+        f=f+cl_check("entire_world_reconstructed",nio_equal(w,pristine),1);
7719:+        let resumed:[]u8=nio_alloc(96);cl_copy(resumed,w);let i:i32=0;

8105:+Section 1 holds the ENTIRE N17 245536-byte packet, including its inner digest.
8106:+Sections 2–10 remain explicit engineering tags, not additional learned state.
8107:+Section 11 is the 96-byte world; section 12 is the 16-byte ingress stream.
8108:+The resulting outer transaction is 245924 bytes. No existing transport/world
8109:+source or original driver was edited. This does not replace the original driver
8110:+with a behavioral learner. The source packet is freshly generated from current
8111:+N17 `state_image_qual_v92.zag`, NOT from any historical receipt or canonical brain.
8112:+
8113:+`ld_commit` validates both layers before persistence. `ld_load` wraps the
8114:+existing outer loader with an additional staging buffer: inner validation must
8115:+pass before any caller-visible packet, world, or ingress bytes are copied.
8116:+Thus atomicity means whole-record validation/publication and unchanged output
8117:+on refusal. It does NOT assert atomic pathname rename or power-loss durability
8118:+beyond the existing file/root sync implementation. Bad fixture writers purposely
8119:+bypass admission to exercise refusal. Torn packet/checkpoint coverage is a
8120:+deterministic truncation fixture, not a newly injected power failure; the original
8121:+native supervisor's partial-write crash/refusal regression was also rerun.
8122:+

10748:+    ns_sha256("LANE_D_REDUCED_CALLER",parent);
10749:+    if(world.len!=96 || ingress.len!=16){return 71;}
10750:+    let rc:i32=cl_checkpoint_begin(out,1,parent,previous);if(rc!=0){return rc;}
10751:+    let at:i32=CL_CHECKPOINT_HEADER;
10752:+    rc=cl_checkpoint_section(out,&at,1,payload);
10753:+    if(rc!=0){return 81;}
10754:+    if(at!=120+payload.len){return 82;}
10755:+    let tag:[]u8=nio_alloc(4);if(tag.len!=4){return 83;}
10756:+    let id:i32=2;
10757:+    while(id<=10){cl_put(tag,0,id as i64);rc=cl_checkpoint_section(out,&at,id,tag);if(rc!=0){nio_free(tag);return 84;}id=id+1;}
10758:+    nio_free(tag);

10760:+    rc=cl_checkpoint_section(out,&at,12,ingress);if(rc!=0){return 86;}
10761:+    rc=cl_checkpoint_finish(out,at);if(cl_is_status(rc)==1){return 87;}return rc;
10762:+}
10763:+
10764:+fn main()i32 {
10765:+    let out:[]u8=nio_alloc(300000);let payload:[]u8=nio_alloc(245536);
10766:+    let parent:[]u8=nio_alloc(32);let previous:[]u8=nio_alloc(32);
10767:+    let world:[]u8=nio_alloc(96);let ingress:[]u8=nio_alloc(16);
10768:+    if(out.len!=300000 || payload.len!=245536 || parent.len!=32 || previous.len!=32 || world.len!=96 || ingress.len!=16){return 70;}
10769:+    let i:i32=0;while(i<payload.len){payload[i]=(i & 255) as u8;i=i+1;}
10770:+    payload[0]=67;payload[1]=76;payload[2]=65;payload[3]=49;
10771:+    i=0;while(i<world.len){world[i]=((i*3) & 255) as u8;i=i+1;}
10772:+    i=0;while(i<ingress.len){ingress[i]=((i*7) & 255) as u8;i=i+1;}
10773:+    ns_sha256("V73_SIX_SLICE_CALLER",parent);
10774:+    let rc:i32=v73_build(out,payload);
10775:+    _zag_print("LANE_D_REDUCED_RC,");cl_number(rc as i64);_zag_println("");
10776:+    nio_free(out);nio_free(payload);nio_free(parent);nio_free(previous);nio_free(world);nio_free(ingress);
10777:+    return (rc<245536) as i32;
10778:+}

11363:+// begin followed by a section write through a caller-owned scalar pointer.
11364:+@import("checkpoint.zag")
11365:+
11366:+fn v73_build(out:[]u8,payload:[]u8)i32 {
11367:+    let parent:[]u8=nio_alloc(32);let previous:[]u8=nio_alloc(32);
11368:+    let world:[]u8=nio_alloc(96);let ingress:[]u8=nio_alloc(16);

11370:+    if(world.len!=96 || ingress.len!=16){return 71;}

11372:+    let at:i32=CL_CHECKPOINT_HEADER;
11373:+    rc=cl_checkpoint_section(out,&at,1,payload);
11374:+    if(rc!=0){return 81;}
11375:+    if(at!=120+payload.len){return 82;}
11376:+    let tag:[]u8=nio_alloc(4);if(tag.len!=4){return 83;}
11377:+    let id:i32=2;
11378:+    while(id<=10){cl_put(tag,0,id as i64);rc=cl_checkpoint_section(out,&at,id,tag);if(rc!=0){nio_free(tag);return 84;}id=id+1;}
11379:+    nio_free(tag);
11380:+    rc=cl_checkpoint_section(out,&at,11,world);if(rc!=0){return 85;}
11381:+    rc=cl_checkpoint_section(out,&at,12,ingress);if(rc!=0){return 86;}
11382:+    rc=cl_checkpoint_finish(out,at);if(cl_is_status(rc)==1){return 87;}return rc;
11383:+}

11387:+    let parent:[]u8=nio_alloc(32);let previous:[]u8=nio_alloc(32);
11388:+    let world:[]u8=nio_alloc(96);let ingress:[]u8=nio_alloc(16);
11389:+    if(out.len!=300000 || payload.len!=245536 || parent.len!=32 || previous.len!=32 || world.len!=96 || ingress.len!=16){return 70;}
11390:+    let i:i32=0;while(i<payload.len){payload[i]=(i & 255) as u8;i=i+1;}
11391:+    payload[0]=67;payload[1]=76;payload[2]=65;payload[3]=49;

12564:+    let out:[]u8=nio_alloc(300000);let n:i32=ld_build(out);let f:i32=cl_check("packet_checkpoint_bytes",n,245924);
12565:+    if(n!=245924){return 1;}
12566:+    let parent:[]u8=nio_alloc(32);let previous:[]u8=nio_alloc(32);ld_parent(parent);
12567:+    if(nio_equal(mode,"write")==1){f=f+cl_check("packet_outer_commit",ld_commit(root,out[0..n]),0);}
12568:+    if(nio_equal(mode,"corrupt")==1){out[120+500]=out[120+500]^1;f=f+cl_check("outer_corrupt_commit_refused",ld_commit(root,out[0..n]),cl_corrupt());f=f+cl_check("corrupt_fixture_write",cl_file_write(root,"checkpoint-1.bin",out[0..n]),0);}
12569:+    if(nio_equal(mode,"inner")==1){
12570:+        out[120+500]=out[120+500]^1;
12571:+        f=f+cl_check("resigned_outer_finish",cl_checkpoint_finish(out,n-32),n);
12572:+        f=f+cl_check("resigned_outer_valid",cl_checkpoint_check(out[0..n],parent,previous,1),0);
12573:+        f=f+cl_check("resigned_inner_invalid",r33cla1_check(out[120..120+245536]),-1);
12574:+        f=f+cl_check("inner_corrupt_commit_refused",ld_commit(root,out[0..n]),cl_corrupt());
12575:+        f=f+cl_check("inner_fixture_write",cl_file_write(root,"checkpoint-1.bin",out[0..n]),0);
12576:+    }
12577:+    if(nio_equal(mode,"torn")==1){f=f+cl_check("torn_fixture_write",cl_file_write(root,"checkpoint-1.bin",out[0..n/2]),0);}
12578:+    nio_free(out);nio_free(parent);nio_free(previous);return (f!=0) as i32;
12579:+}
12580:+fn ld_read(root:i64,refuse:i32)i32 {
12581:+    let out:[]u8=nio_alloc(300000);let before:[]u8=nio_alloc(300000);let expected:[]u8=nio_alloc(300000);
12582:+    let i:i32=0;while(i<out.len){out[i]=165;i=i+1;}cl_copy(before,out);
12583:+    let n:i32=ld_load(root,out);let f:i32=0;

12585:+    if(refuse==1){f=f+cl_check("bad_packet_transaction_refused",cl_is_status(n),1);f=f+cl_check("refusal_preserves_entire_output",nio_equal(out,before),1);}
12586:+    else{
12587:+        f=f+cl_check("fresh_packet_outer_load",n,245924);
12588:+        if(n==245924){

12590:+            f=f+cl_check("fresh_exact_entire_checkpoint",nio_equal(out[0..n],expected[0..e]),1);
12591:+            let packet:[]u8=cl_checkpoint_get(out[0..n],1);let source:[]u8=nio_alloc(245536);ld_packet(source);
12592:+            f=f+cl_check("fresh_exact_complete_packet",nio_equal(packet,source),1);f=f+cl_check("fresh_inner_valid",r33cla1_check(packet),0);
12593:+            let image:[]u8=r33cla1_extract(packet);let credit:[]u8=r27i4_get(image,32);
12594:+            f=f+cl_check("pending_credit_section_bytes",credit.len,48);

12597:+            let world:[]u8=cl_checkpoint_get(out[0..n],11);f=f+cl_check("world_pending_retained",cl_u32(world,44),2);
12598:+            let pristine:[]u8=nio_alloc(96);cw_init(pristine,1717);let a:CWOutcome=cw_step(pristine,1);let b:CWOutcome=cw_step(pristine,4);

12601:+            f=f+cl_check("ingress_exact_retained",nio_equal(cl_checkpoint_get(out[0..n],12),cl_checkpoint_get(expected[0..e],12)),1);
12602:+            nio_free(source);nio_free(pristine);nio_free(resumed);

12604:+    }

12606:+}

13193:+// Validate inner integrity before copying ANY world, ingress, or packet bytes
13194:+// into caller-visible output. Outer loader already stages its own transaction.
13195:+fn ld_commit(root:i64,b:[]u8)i32 {
13196:+    let parent:[]u8=nio_alloc(32);let previous:[]u8=nio_alloc(32);ld_parent(parent);
13197:+    let rc:i32=cl_checkpoint_check(b,parent,previous,1);
13198:+    if(rc==0 && r33cla1_check(cl_checkpoint_get(b,1))!=0){rc=cl_corrupt();}
13199:+    if(rc==0){rc=cl_checkpoint_commit(root,b,parent,previous,1);}
13200:+    nio_free(parent);nio_free(previous);return rc;
13201:+}
13202:+fn ld_load(root:i64,out:[]u8)i32 {
13203:+    let stage:[]u8=nio_alloc(out.len);let parent:[]u8=nio_alloc(32);let previous:[]u8=nio_alloc(32);ld_parent(parent);
13204:+    let n:i32=cl_checkpoint_load(root,1,parent,previous,stage);
13205:+    if(n<0 || cl_is_status(n)==1){nio_free(stage);nio_free(parent);nio_free(previous);return n;}
13206:+    if(n<245536){nio_free(stage);nio_free(parent);nio_free(previous);return cl_corrupt();}
13207:+    let packet:[]u8=cl_checkpoint_get(stage[0..n],1);
13208:+    if(r33cla1_check(packet)!=0 || cl_checkpoint_get(stage[0..n],11).len!=96 || cl_checkpoint_get(stage[0..n],12).len!=16){nio_free(stage);nio_free(parent);nio_free(previous);return cl_corrupt();}
13209:+    cl_copy(out[0..n],stage[0..n]);nio_free(stage);nio_free(parent);nio_free(previous);return n;
13210:+}
13211:+fn ld_write(root:i64,mode:[]u8)i32 {
13212:+    let out:[]u8=nio_alloc(300000);let n:i32=ld_build(out);let f:i32=cl_check("packet_checkpoint_bytes",n,245924);
13213:+    if(n!=245924){return 1;}

13216:+    if(nio_equal(mode,"corrupt")==1){out[120+500]=out[120+500]^1;f=f+cl_check("outer_corrupt_commit_refused",ld_commit(root,out[0..n]),cl_corrupt());f=f+cl_check("corrupt_fixture_write",cl_file_write(root,"checkpoint-1.bin",out[0..n]),0);}
13217:+    if(nio_equal(mode,"inner")==1){

13220:+        f=f+cl_check("resigned_outer_valid",cl_checkpoint_check(out[0..n],parent,previous,1),0);
13221:+        f=f+cl_check("resigned_inner_invalid",r33cla1_check(out[120..120+245536]),-1);
13222:+        f=f+cl_check("inner_corrupt_commit_refused",ld_commit(root,out[0..n]),cl_corrupt());

13224:+    }

13226:+    nio_free(out);nio_free(parent);nio_free(previous);return (f!=0) as i32;
13227:+}

13233:+    if(refuse==1){f=f+cl_check("bad_packet_transaction_refused",cl_is_status(n),1);f=f+cl_check("refusal_preserves_entire_output",nio_equal(out,before),1);}
13234:+    else{

13239:+            let packet:[]u8=cl_checkpoint_get(out[0..n],1);let source:[]u8=nio_alloc(245536);ld_packet(source);
